# Source outline for the four database books

Generated from PDF bookmarks. This is an inventory, **not evidence that a section has been read or taught**. Page numbers are physical PDF pages. Front matter, contents pages, and indexes are filtered where they add no learning content; selected non-instructional entries are kept and marked `reviewed` or `not applicable` (including the *Database Internals* bibliography and Redis resource appendix). A checkbox is complete only after reading the source section and mapping its learning content to specific headings in both VI and EN notes. Inventory counts below are retained PDF bookmarks, not note-topic counts. Thirty-one useful body-text learning units have no PDF bookmark; each is labeled inline and excluded from the bookmark count: seven in *Database Internals* (RAMP, PBFT View Changes, PostgreSQL versus `fsync()`, Bypassing the Kernel Page Cache, Quorums and incomplete writes, SSTable-attached secondary indexes, and RIFL completion objects/leases); eleven in *High Performance MySQL* (Local Shared-Memory and Distributed Memory Caches, Invalidation on Read, and nine backup-script blocks: argument parsing, connection, shutdown/start, database/table inventory, lock/flush, binlog rotation, coordinate capture, dump, and copy); ten in *Redis in Action* (multiple executable tasks/task priorities, delayed-task polling/safe promotion, delayed-task priority scheduling, fair semaphores on 32-bit platforms, four chat-session operations, bounded follower fan-out, and status deletion/zombie timeline IDs); and three in *PostgreSQL Internals 14* (TOAST storage strategies/row-size target, Locks in Shared Memory, and Join Selectivity and Foreign Keys). These are body subsections, not additional PDF bookmarks.

## Book-to-topic map

Each source book has one home topic. The 31 unbookmarked body sections are integrated into the related VI/EN notes in that topic; they do not create extra topics or standalone notes. Topic-guide order is the reading path, while the tables below retain the book's own chapter and page order. Topic note counts include a few cross-source foundations: three data-model/query notes synthesized from MySQL and PostgreSQL chapters sit at the start of Database Systems, and PostgreSQL has one additional operations note based on current official documentation.

| Source book | Home topic | Topic-guide note pairs | Notes outside this book's chapters |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| *Database Internals* | `database-systems` — Hệ thống cơ sở dữ liệu / Database Systems | 29 | 3 cross-source foundation notes |
| *High Performance MySQL* | `mysql-design-performance-operations` — MySQL: Thiết kế, tối ưu và vận hành | 25 | — |
| *Redis in Action* | `redis-data-structures-applications` — Redis: Cấu trúc dữ liệu và ứng dụng thực tế | 19 | — |
| *PostgreSQL Internals 14* | `postgresql-transactions-queries-storage` — PostgreSQL: Giao dịch, truy vấn và lưu trữ | 16 | 1 official-documentation operations note |
